Conclusion: Rubrics on Trial 证明了 query-only 的自动 rubric 构建是可行且有效的，能够在不依赖外部标注和训练的情况下生成更有判别力的评估标准。

Abstract: Rubrics provide structured, fine-grained signals for training and evaluating large language models (LLMs). Yet reliable query-specific rubrics are difficult to construct. Existing approaches often derive supervision from human-written rubrics, preference data, or sampled responses. Direct query-to-rubric generation avoids these resources, but provides no explicit check that a plausible rubric is useful. Such a rubric may fail to distinguish answer quality, reward an optional style, or penalize a valid alternative strategy. We introduce Rubrics on Trial, a query-only framework that evolves a rubric set from an empty set without external annotations or model training. It derives supervision solely from synthetic rubric-conditioned response pairs and validates each proposed rubric before adding it, screening out non-discriminative, over-specific, and style-only candidate rubrics. Experiments across five preference benchmark suites demonstrate the effectiveness of Rubrics on Trial, which achieves the best average accuracy and leads on six of seven evaluation sets.

</details>


### [142] [Digital Pantheon: Simulating and Auditing Coalition Formation with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15095)
*Dylan Van Mulders,Matthias Bogaert,Dirk Van den Poel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: The formation of political coalitions is a complex negotiation driven by both concrete policy objectives and deep-seated ideological convictions. While Large Language Models (LLMs) open new avenues for computational political science, the neutrality and helpfulness biases instilled by Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) prevent them from sustaining steadfast partisan behaviour. We present a multi-agent framework that reconciles factual grounding with ideological alignment by combining Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG): DPO instils aggressive party-specific personas, while a per-party RAG pipeline keeps each agent bounded to its official manifesto. We operationalize the framework on the 2019 Flemish election, deploying the partisan agents in a hub-and-spoke negotiation arbitrated by a formateur. To make the emergent negotiation interpretable, we introduce a Multi-Layered Information Lineage Topology (MILT) that traces every clause in the final agreement back to its manifesto origin and classifies it into five provenance states, a Coalition Influence Score (CIS) that aggregates these traceable contributions to identify which party shaped the agreement, and a real-world grounding pass that benchmarks each simulated provision against the historically adopted coalition agreement. Across three independent simulations the framework yields a stable winner and ranking (N-VA ahead of CD\&V and Open Vld), and manifesto-anchored lineage reliably predicts real-world materialization whereas hallucinated content does not. The result is a transparent, scalable testbed for the ex-ante exploration of party compatibility and formateur-mediated compromise.

</details>


### [143] [Grokipedia vs Wikipedia: An LLM-Based Audit of Political Neutrality along Ideologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15146)
*Filippos Vlahos,Guillaume Bied,Tijl De Bie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Online encyclopedias shape political opinion and, through it, democratic discourse. In late 2025, Grokipedia was released, an encyclopedia written entirely by the LLM Grok. One motivation behind the project was to provide an unbiased alternative to Wikipedia, which has faced accusations of "left-wing" and "liberal" bias. But does an encyclopedia written by an LLM deliver greater neutrality, or does it simply embed a different ideology? We conduct a large-scale political bias study on Grokipedia and Wikipedia, analysing 1,394 article pairs describing members of government for neutrality along nine expert-coded ideology dimensions employing four LLM judges, Grok, Claude, Mistral, and DeepSeek. As the LLMs could themselves be biased, we also investigate patterns in their judgments. We find all LLM-judges, including Grok, to rate Grokipedia less neutral than Wikipedia. Both encyclopedias are rated as portraying politicians favourably overall, but towards different ideological groups. Grokipedia particularly favours economically right-wing politicians and penalises socially liberal ones, while Wikipedia is rated as favourably biased towards the latter.

</details>


### [144] [Linear representations of grammaticality in neural language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15175)
*Jane Li,Najoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出用内部表征而非概率分数评估神经语言模型的语法性知识，发现多种预训练模型的句子表征中稳定编码了语法性维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多依赖模型给语法句赋予更高概率这一证据，但概率受词频、合理性、世界知识等因素影响，难以单独证明模型具备语法知识。因此作者希望寻找不依赖字符串概率的评估框架。

Method: 作者使用 mass-mean probing 分析神经语言模型的内部句子表征，检验语法句与非语法句是否在表征空间中系统性分离；同时控制或比较与语法性相关的其他句子属性，并测试跨语法现象与跨语言的泛化能力。

Result: 多种预训练神经语言模型在表征空间中表现出清晰的语法性分离；这种分离不能完全由词汇频率、句子合理性、世界知识等替代性句子因素解释，并且可在多类语法现象及一定程度的跨语言设置中泛化。

Conclusion: 语法性在当代神经语言模型中构成一个较为一致且稳健的表征维度。该研究为语言模型是否具有句法知识的争论提供了新证据，并提出了对概率评估的补充性语法能力评估方法。

Abstract: Whether neural language models (NLMs) possess the ability to distinguish strings on the basis of their grammaticality remains a debated topic in the computational linguistics literature. Existing evidence has largely relied on probability-based measures, testing whether models assign higher probabilities to grammatical than ungrammatical strings. However, probability comparisons have been criticized as a measure for grammatical knowledge based on the assumption that grammaticality is inherently entangled with likelihood. Model-assigned probability is a function of many related sentence properties, such as lexical frequency, plausibility, and world knowledge. In this work, we move beyond probability-based evaluations and investigate whether grammaticality is encoded in the internal representations of NLMs. Using mass-mean probing, we test whether grammatical and ungrammatical sentences are systematically separated in representational space. We further examine the extent to which these representations are independent of sentence properties that are correlated with grammaticality, as well as their generalization across grammatical phenomena and languages. Our results provide evidence that grammaticality is robustly encoded in sentence representations of a wide range of pretrained NLMs, yielding clear representational separation on the dimension of grammaticality that cannot be fully explained by alternative sentence-level factors. Moreover, this encoding generalizes across a broad range of grammatical phenomena and to some degree, across languages, suggesting that grammaticality constitutes a coherent representational dimension in contemporary NLMs. These findings contribute new evidence to debates about the nature of syntactic knowledge in language models and offer a complementary framework for evaluating grammatical competence that is not dependent on string probabilities alone.

</details>


### [145] [T^2MLR: Transformer with Temporal Middle-Layer Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15178)
*Ziyang Cai,Xingyu Zhu,Yihe Dong,Yinghui He,Sanjeev Arora*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: T2MLR通过在Transformer解码时引入中间层的时间递归缓存，使上一token的中间层表征直接影响当前token的早期层，从而增强潜在推理状态的跨步持续性，并在预训练、微调和现有模型改造中取得优于匹配基线的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自回归Transformer每一步都需要将丰富的隐藏计算压缩为离散token输出，导致中间推理状态难以在解码时间维度上稳定保留，限制了复杂、多步推理能力。作者希望在不显著增加推理开销的情况下，让抽象的中间计算状态跨token延续。

Method: 提出Transformers with Temporal Middle-Layer Recurrence（T2MLR）：缓存前一个token位置的中间层表示，并将其融合到当前token位置的较早层中。该递归路径主要作用于局部中间层块，而非整个网络；同时可将该机制改造到已有预训练Transformer中，并通过短暂微调适配。

Result: 在自然语言预训练和多跳推理微调任务中，T2MLR持续优于数据规模和参数量匹配的Transformer基线。实验还发现，仅对约20%的中间层局部块施加递归，往往优于全层递归。对现有1.7B预训练Transformer进行递归路径改造并短暂微调后，数学推理能力显著提升。

Conclusion: 有效的Transformer潜在推理不一定需要像既有方法那样对所有层进行循环计算；更有针对性的中间层时间递归即可更强地促进跨步推理状态保留，并具备较低开销和较好的实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Transformer reasoning is limited by autoregressive decoding, which repeat edly compresses rich hidden computation through token space and makes it difficult for intermediate reasoning states to persist across time. We in troduce Transformers with Temporal Middle-Layer Recurrence (T2MLR), a transformers-based latent reasoning architecture that fuses a cached middle layer representation from the previous token directly into an earlier layer of the current token position, enabling abstract intermediate computation to persist across decoding steps with little inference overhead. Across natural-language pretraining and multi-hop reasoning finetuning, T2MLR consistently outperforms data- and parameter-matched Transformer base lines. Moreover, applying recurrence to only a localized middle-layer block (as little as 20% of the network) often outperforms full-layer recurrence. Im portantly, T2MLR does not require pretraining from scratch: retrofitting the recurrent pathway into an existing pretrained 1.7B Transformer and briefly finetuning substantially improves math reasoning, lowering the barrier to practical adoption. These results suggest that effective latent reasoning in Transformers does not require looping over all layers as in previous works, but can instead emerge more strongly from targeted middle-layer recurrence.

</details>


### [146] [Mask-Aware Policy Gradients for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15200)
*Haran Raajesh,Kulin Shah,Adam Klivans,Philipp Krähenbühl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出将MDLM生成过程建模为包含“token预测”和“位置重掩码”两阶段动作的MDP，并同时优化两类策略梯度项，从而提升推理与代码任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已被证明能提升大语言模型推理能力，但在Masked Diffusion Language Models中应用困难，核心原因是其对数似然估计不可tractable。已有方法只近似建模token预测，忽略生成过程中位置被解除遮蔽/重新遮蔽的顺序决策，因此优化目标不完整。

Method: 作者观察到MDLM每一步生成包含两个决策：在被mask位置填入哪些token，以及哪些位置需要重新mask。论文将该过程形式化为两阶段动作马尔可夫决策过程，并推导出策略梯度可自然分解为token项与masking项；随后联合优化这两个项。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准上取得先进结果，包括GSM8K达到87.1%，MBPP达到53.4%。

Conclusion: MDLM的强化学习优化不应仅关注token预测，还应显式建模和优化masking/重掩码决策。联合优化token与masking策略能更充分匹配MDLM生成机制，并显著提升推理与编程任务性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has proven effective for improving reasoning in large language models, but extending it to Masked Diffusion Language Models (MDLMs) remains challenging due to the intractability of the log-likelihood estimation. Existing approaches approximate this log-likelihood by modeling only the token predictions, ignoring the order in which positions are unmasked during generation. We observe that MDLM generation involves two decisions at each step: what tokens to place at each masked position and which positions to remask. We formalize this as a two-stage action MDP, showing that the policy gradient naturally decomposes into a token term and a masking term. Combining optimization of both terms leads to state-of-the-art outcomes on mathematical reasoning and coding benchmarks, with scores of 87.1% on GSM8K and 53.4% on MBPP.

</details>


### [147] [Expanding the Lexicon of Ge'ez Based African Languages: A Comparative Study of Amharic and Tigrinya](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15209)
*Hailay Kidu Teklehaymanot,Debela Desalegn Yadeta,Wolfgang Nejdl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 VEXMLM：在 XLM-R 基础上扩展 Ge'ez 文字子词词表，并通过继续预训练与下游微调提升低资源非拉丁文字语言表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语预训练语言模型在低资源、非拉丁文字语言上性能下降，主要源于以拉丁文字为中心训练的分词器导致高 OOV 率和过度子词切分，尤其影响 Amharic、Tigrinya 等 Ge'ez 文字语言。

Method: 针对 Amharic 和 Tigrinya 语料训练语言特定 SentencePiece 分词器，从中提取 30,000 个 Ge'ez 文字子词扩展 XLM-R 词表；新词嵌入通过原 XLM-R 分词器下构成子词嵌入的平均值初始化。随后进行两阶段训练：扩展词表上的掩码语言模型继续预训练，以及在 QA、NER、情感分析任务上监督微调。

Result: 在 Amharic/Tigrinya QA 上，VEXMLM 达到 87.0 EM / 90.0 F1，显著优于 XLM-R 的 66.0/78.0 和 Glot500 的 74.0/78.0；情感分析准确率达到 80.0%，高于 XLM-R 的 77.0% 与 Glot500 的 46.0%；NER 中，OOV token 实体准确率从 81.4% 提升至 94.3%。

Conclusion: 词表扩展、合理嵌入初始化和继续预训练能有效缓解非拉丁文字低资源语言的分词问题，并将 Amharic/Tigrinya 上的收益迁移到更多相关低资源语言，在内在分词指标和外在任务性能上均有改进。

Abstract: Multilingual pre-trained language models (PLMs) exhibit degraded performance on low-resource, non-Latin-script languages, driven by high out-of-vocabulary (OOV) rates and excessive subword fragmentation that result from Latin-script-centric tokenizer training. We introduce VEXMLM, a vocabulary-extended variant of XLM-R targeting the two highest-resource Ge'ez-script languages, Amharic and Tigrinya, and further evaluated on 17 additional low-resource African languages (19 total). We train a language-specific SentencePiece tokenizer on curated Amharic and Tigrinya monolingual corpora, extend XLM-R's vocabulary with 30,000 Ge'ez-script subwords derived from this tokenizer, and initialize their embeddings by averaging the embeddings of their constituent subwords under XLM-R's original tokenizer. VEXMLM is trained in two stages: (1) continued masked language modeling over the extended vocabulary on the curated corpora, and (2) supervised fine-tuning on question answering (QA), named entity recognition (NER), and sentiment analysis (SA). On Amharic/Tigrinya QA, VEXMLM achieves 87.0 EM /90.0 F1, versus 66.0 EM/78.0 F1 for XLM-R and 74.0 EM/ 78.0 F1 for Glot500. On SA, VEXMLM reaches 80.0\% accuracy versus 77.0\% (XLM-R) and 46.0\% (Glot500). On NER, VEXMLM raises OOV-token entity accuracy from 81.4\% to 94.3\%, averaged over 11 of the 19 evaluated languages for which OOV analysis was possible. Our contributions are: (i) a vocabulary-extension and embedding-initialization procedure tailored to Ge'ez script; (ii) a two-stage training strategy under which vocabulary and continued-pretraining gains on Amharic/Tigrinya transfer to 17 typologically related, unaugmented African languages; and (iii) an evaluation spanning both intrinsic tokenization metrics (vocabulary coverage, fertility, OOV rate) and extrinsic task performance across all 19 languages.

</details>


### [148] [In-Place Tokenizer Expansion for Pre-trained LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15232)
*Jimmy T. H. Smith,Tarek Dakhran,Alberto Cabrera,Simon S. Lee,Paul Pak,Aditya Tadimeti,Tim Seyde,Maxime Labonne,Alexander Amini,Mathias Lechner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种对已预训练模型进行原地分词器扩展的方法，在尽量保持原模型能力的同时减少新增语言的 token 碎片化，从而提升端侧多语言解码效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定于预训练初期的 tokenizer 会按当时语料分布分配词表容量；当后续部署语言需求变化时，新加入或低覆盖语言往往被切分成更多 token，导致延迟、计算量和能耗上升。端侧小模型又受嵌入矩阵与 LM head 带宽影响，难以直接使用超大词表，因此需要一种低风险、低成本的词表升级方案。

Method: 作者在原有 BPE tokenizer 的基础上，使用多语言语料继续执行 BPE merge，使原 token 大多保持不变，并保证新 token 可精确分解为原 tokenizer 的子 token。对于模型参数，保留原 embedding 行不变，新 token embedding 用其源子 token embedding 的均值初始化；随后采用两阶段适配：先仅训练 embedding，再进行全模型继续预训练。

Result: 该方法应用于 LFM2-8B-A1B 的继续预训练 checkpoint，帮助得到带 128K tokenizer 的 LFM2.5-8B-A1B。扩展后 tokenizer 在 Hindi、Vietnamese 上分别约减少 2.4×、2.6× token 数，在 Thai 上最高达 4.0×。结合更大词表带来的逐 token 成本，作者估计这些语言在参考设备上的逐字符解码速度提升约 2.2–3.7×。

Conclusion: 分词器扩展可以在保留源模型质量的同时显著改善新增语言的编码效率，尤其适合受词表规模与带宽约束的端侧模型。论文还开源了模型权重和扩展 tokenizer，并报告了形成最终方案过程中的负面实验发现。

Abstract: A tokenizer fixed at the start of pre-training allocates vocabulary in proportion to the pre-training corpus, reflecting the deployment priorities at that time. When those priorities shift, languages added later are split into many more tokens per word, which can raise latency, compute, and energy consumption for users of those languages. Cloud models can afford a broad vocabulary because the embedding and LM-head matrices are a small fraction of their parameters. On a compact model those matrices are a material share of per-token decode bandwidth, so on-device models ship small vocabularies and accept fragmentation outside a fixed language set. We present tokenizer expansion, an in-place recipe for upgrading a pre-trained model's tokenizer when the model producer controls its design. We continue the existing tokenizer's BPE merges on a multilingual corpus, so most source tokens carry over unchanged as single tokens and every new token has an exact decomposition into source tokens. We copy the carried-over embedding rows unchanged and initialize new rows as the mean of their source sub-token embeddings. A two-stage adaptation, embedding-only training then full-model continued pre-training, recovers source-checkpoint quality. We apply the recipe to a continued pre-trained checkpoint of LFM2-8B-A1B, an 8B-parameter Mixture-of-Experts model, to help produce LFM2.5-8B-A1B with a 128K tokenizer. The expanded tokenizer encodes Hindi and Vietnamese in roughly $2.4\times$ and $2.6\times$ fewer tokens than the source (up to $4.0\times$ on Thai). Combining these reductions with the measured per-token cost of the larger vocabulary, we estimate a $2.2$-$3.7\times$ per-character decode speedup for these languages across our reference devices. We release the model weights and the expanded tokenizer, and report the negative findings that shaped the recipe.

</details>


### [149] [Language Identification via Compositional Data Analysis: A Linear-Time Classifier Based on Log-Ratio Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15238)
*Paul-Andrei Pogăcean,Sanda-Maria Avram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于组合数据分析的语言识别方法，将字符与二元字符频率视为单纯形上的组合向量，并通过CLR变换使用Aitchison几何进行距离度量，实现高效、可解释的语言识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言识别方法主要依赖神经网络或统计n-gram模型。神经方法通常计算资源消耗较高；传统频率方法虽高效，但常用距离度量未必适合频率分布这类组合数据，因此需要一种既低资源又符合数据几何性质的替代方案。

Method: 作者将字符unigram和bigram频率分布建模为受单纯形约束的组合向量，使用中心化对数比（CLR）变换将其双射映射到欧氏空间中的零和子空间，使欧氏距离对应Aitchison距离；同时结合Laplace平滑处理稀疏性，并构建基于CLR变换特征的语言识别流程，在六种语言上评估。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在不同文本长度下具有稳健准确率，尤其在较长文本序列上表现较强。

Conclusion: 组合表示与CLR/Aitchison几何可作为一种确定性、计算高效且可解释的语言识别方案，特别适合资源受限或强调模型透明性的应用场景。

Abstract: Language identification is commonly addressed using either neural architectures or statistical n-gram models. Neural approaches typically require substantial computational resources, whereas classical frequency-based methods offer efficient linear-time performance, but rely on distance metrics that are not always appropriate for compositional data.
  This work models character and bigram frequency distributions as compositional vectors constrained to the simplex and mapped via the centered log-ratio (CLR) transformation bijectively onto the $(D-1)$-dimensional zero-sum subspace of $\mathbb{R}^D$, where Euclidean distances correspond to Aitchison distances. A pipeline is proposed, combining CLR-transformed unigram and bigram features with Laplace smoothing to address sparsity. The method is evaluated on six languages.
  Experimental results show that the proposed approach achieves robust accuracy across different text lengths, with strong performance for longer sequences. These findings indicate that compositional representations provide a deterministic and computationally efficient alternative for language identification, particularly in settings where interpretability and low resource consumption are essential.

</details>


### [150] [TikStance: A Multimodal and Hierarchical Dataset for Multi-target Stance Analysis in TikTok Political Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15240)
*Yazhi Zhang,Fuqiang Niu,Bowen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Political discourse has increasingly moved to short-video platforms, yet computational analysis of such content remains constrained by the scarcity of datasets that jointly preserve audiovisual information and hierarchical conversations. Here we present TikStance, a multimodal and context-aware dataset comprising 161 videos and 13,876 comments from TikTok, designed for stance detection in political discussions. The dataset covers three major political figures in the 2024 U.S. election cycle--Donald Trump, Joe Biden, and Kamala Harris--with content collected between September 2023 and January 2025. Each discussion unit links a host video and its metadata to a parent-linked comment tree, enabling stance analysis within both audiovisual and conversational context. Each item was independently labeled by three annotators using a three-class scheme (Favor, Against, None) for video-to-target and comment-to-target stance; items with disagreement were re-annotated, and the final Krippendorff's \(α\) reached 0.743, 0.723, and 0.722 for the Trump, Biden, and Harris subsets, respectively. Descriptive analysis further reveals target-dependent differences in stance distributions and conversational depth, with nested replies accounting for 23.3\% of all comments. By combining multi-target coverage, hierarchical conversations, and reliable multi-level human annotations, TikStance supports research in multimodal stance detection, political communication, computational social science, and context-aware natural language processing.

</details>


### [151] [Beyond the Leaderboard: Design Lessons for Trustworthy Multimodal VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15241)
*Sushant Gautam,Vajira Thambawita,Michael A. Riegler,Pål Halvorsen,Steven A. Hicks*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文以 MediaEval Medico 2025 的回顾性胃肠内镜案例为对象，比较九个多模态医疗问答系统，指出高性能答案生成并不必然意味着可靠、忠实的临床解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗多模态 AI 需要同时融合视觉与文本证据，并在临床场景中保持可靠性、可解释性与鲁棒性；现有评估常偏重答案正确或词面重合，难以充分衡量推理是否忠实、完整。

Method: 作者分析九个已记录系统在问答与解释质量上的设计选择，关注参数高效微调、预训练骨干模型、结构化推理、显式证据 grounding、异构问题类型表现，以及数据治理、鲁棒性和校准评估等方面。

Result: 参数高效适配预训练模型可取得较强挑战赛表现，但答案层面的提升并不稳定转化为忠实且完整的临床推理；采用结构化推理和显式证据关联的方法在不同问题类型上表现更可靠，但证据主要是相关性分析而非消融实验。

Conclusion: 研究主张医疗多模态 AI 评估应超越词面重合，引入标准化的证据链接解释、泄漏感知的数据治理，以及轻量级鲁棒性与校准检查，以支持更可信的多模态医疗 AI。

Abstract: Healthcare multimodal AI must combine visual and textual evidence while remaining reliable and interpretable. Using MediaEval Medico 2025 as a retrospective GI endoscopy case study, we analyze design choices across nine documented systems for question answering and explanation quality. Parameter-efficient adaptation of pretrained backbones provides strong challenge performance, but answer-level gains do not consistently translate into faithful and complete clinical reasoning. Methods enforcing structured reasoning and explicit grounding show more reliable behavior across heterogeneous question types, although the evidence is correlational rather than ablation-based. These results motivate evaluation beyond lexical overlap, standardized evidence-linked explanations, leakage-aware data governance, and lightweight robustness and calibration checks. The findings support trustworthy multimodal healthcare AI based on data fusion, explainability, and resilient evaluation.

</details>


### [152] [SciDiagramEdit: Learning to Edit Scientific Diagrams from Paper Revisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15272)
*Yasheng Sun,Zezi Zeng,Yifan Yang,Chong Luo,Wenyi Wang,Ziwei Liu,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 SciDiagramEdit：一个基于论文自然修订历史的科学图表编辑基准与技能演化框架，用于根据自然语言指令自动编辑可交互的矢量源文件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研论文图表修改频繁且耗时，涉及重标注、重排版、样式调整等操作；但科学图表由示意图、曲线图、照片、标题、箭头等异构元素组成，语义密集且视觉规范严格，使自然语言驱动的自动编辑非常困难。

Method: 作者从 arXiv 版本历史中挖掘论文图表的修改前/修改后配对数据，并以作者真实修订意图作为监督信号；系统在可编辑矢量源上操作，使用户可检查和共同编辑图元；同时采用 agentic learning 的技能演化机制，由 proposer 基于多轮执行轨迹持续优化代理的技能规范。

Result: 随着技能在多个 epoch 中演化，代理在保留验证集上的图表编辑准确率逐步提升，表明从自然论文修订中学习能够有效改进指令驱动的图表编辑能力。

Conclusion: SciDiagramEdit 证明了自然论文修订历史可以作为训练信号，用于构建面向科学图表的自然语言编辑基准和可迭代提升的智能编辑代理。

Abstract: Editing the figures in a research paper is a routine and time-consuming part of everyday research practice: authors relabel components, rearrange panels, and restyle visuals as they revise their manuscripts. Automating this editing workflow under a natural-language instruction, however, is challenging, because a scientific figure is a dense infographic in which heterogeneous visual elements such as schematics, plots, photos, captions, and arrows are composed under a tight visual grammar to advance a specific argument. To address this, we present SciDiagramEdit, a benchmark and skill-evolution framework that learns from natural paper revisions and operates on the figure's editable vector source, where users can inspect and co-edit individual primitives alongside the agent. Our benchmark mines before/after figure pairs from arXiv version histories, each grounded in the authors' own revision intent. To accommodate the diversity of editing instructions, we adopt agentic learning via skill evolution: an agentic proposer continually refines the agent's skill specification from execution traces over multiple epochs. The resulting skill progressively lifts edit accuracy on a held-out validation set, providing evidence that natural paper revisions are an effective training signal for instruction-driven figure editing.

</details>


### [153] [Partition, Prompt, Aggregate: Statistical Self-Consistency in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15277)
*Patrik Wolf,Thomas Kleine Buening,Andreas Krause,Celestine Mendler-Dünner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在将上下文学习视为条件推断时，是否满足全概率公式所要求的统计自洽性；结果发现主流模型普遍存在一致性违背。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如果LLM的上下文学习确实是在估计条件分布，那么其输出应满足基本概率恒等式。作者希望检验模型在不同粒度子群体划分下，能否将条件估计一致地聚合为总体边际估计。

Method: 使用二叉树作为评估框架，将总体递归划分为更细粒度的子群体；对各子群体描述进行提示并获取模型估计，再按先验权重聚合回总体估计，比较不同划分粒度下的结果。同时在多个任务领域、前沿模型、树结构和估计任务变体中进行测试，并深入分析persona prompting。

Result: 实验显示LLM广泛违反统计自洽性。细粒度子群体响应聚合得到的总体估计，往往比直接询问总体得到的估计更接近人类参考数据；作者将这一现象称为“macro fallacy”。该现象在不同树结构和任务设置下持续存在，并可通过隐式提示部分恢复。

Conclusion: LLM可能具备相关的子群体知识，但不能可靠地将这些知识传播到总体层面的估计中。统计自洽性因此可作为一种无需参考标签、尚未充分利用的LLM评估标准。

Abstract: In-context learning is commonly interpreted as a form of conditional inference, in which the prompt specifies a context and the model's output is treated as an estimate of the corresponding conditional distribution. If this interpretation holds, then LLM estimates should satisfy basic probabilistic identities. In particular, the law of total probability asserts that prior-weighted conditional distributions aggregate into population-level marginals over any valid partition of the population. In this work, we investigate to what extent LLM estimates adhere to this self-consistency principle. We use binary trees as an evaluation scaffold to recursively partition a population into increasingly fine-grained subpopulations. We then prompt LLMs with verbalized subpopulation descriptions in context, aggregate the resulting estimates back into population-level estimates, and compare them across partitions of varying granularity. Applying this protocol across problem domains and state-of-the-art frontier models, we show widespread violations of basic consistency properties. An in-depth study of persona prompting reveals a pattern we call the macro fallacy: estimates reconstructed from more fine-grained subpopulation responses are often better aligned with human reference data than direct population-level estimates. This effect persists across variations in tree structure and estimation task, and can be partially recovered through implicit prompting. Together, these findings suggest that models possess relevant subpopulation knowledge but do not reliably propagate it into aggregate estimates. This gap establishes statistical self-consistency as an unsaturated, reference-free criterion for evaluating LLMs.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Enhanced Feedback Mechanisms for Resource-Efficient Incremental Redundancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14247)
*Mustafa Cemil Coşkun,Ahmed Elkelesh,Avijit Mandal,Homa Esfahanizadeh*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文针对IR-HARQ中传统粗粒度反馈导致的冗余过量、无效解码与时延增加问题，提出基于信道统计和首传可靠性信息的增强反馈与调度机制，以预测所需最小附加冗余并提升两次传输内成功解码概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增量冗余可通过多次传输分散编码比特来降低误码率，但传统停等式HARQ反馈粒度较粗，常造成重传资源过度配置、触发不必要译码尝试，并增加端到端时延，因此需要更精细的反馈和资源调度机制。

Method: 论文研究两类互补策略：其一，利用信道统计学习从信道质量到最小冗余预算的一次或两次传输映射，并推导HARQ误差概率的可达可靠性下界；其二，提出实现感知的早期反馈机制，基于首次传输的可靠性信息在译码前逐码字判断是否已可译、需要多少额外冗余版本，或是否应进行速率自适应。

Result: 在极化码IR-HARQ数值实验中，通过合理选择第二次传输冗余量，可在较宽SNR范围内接近所推导下界，并最多节省约60%的重传规模。5G NR LDPC链路级仿真显示，两类预测器均达到约96%的高准确率，并提高了最多两次传输机会内成功解码的概率。

Conclusion: 增强反馈与预测式冗余调度能够显著提升IR-HARQ资源利用效率，降低不必要重传和译码开销，同时改善低时延可靠通信中的解码成功率与调度精度。

Abstract: Incremental redundancy (IR) can reduce error rates by spreading coded bits across multiple transmission attempts. However, conventional stop-and-wait operation with coarse feedback often over-provisions retransmissions, triggers unnecessary decoding attempts, and increases end-to-end latency. This paper develops enhanced feedback and scheduling mechanisms that predict the additional redundancy needed for successful decoding and allocate only the required resources. We study two complementary strategies. First, using channel statistics, we learn a one- or two-shot mapping from channel quality to the minimum redundancy budget. As a byproduct, we derive an achievable reliability lower bound on the error probability of hybrid automatic repeat request (HARQ) systems. Numerical results with polar-coded IR-HARQ scheme show that the bound can be closely approached by appropriately selecting the second-transmission redundancy over a wide SNR range with savings up to 60\% in retransmission size. Second, we propose a realization-aware early-feedback mechanism that uses first-transmission reliability information to make per-codeword decisions before decoding: whether the codeword is already decodable, if not, how many additional redundancy versions are needed, or whether decoding is unlikely and rate adaptation is preferable. Link-level simulations with 5G NR LDPC codes show that both predictors achieve high accuracy (about 96\% in our study), increasing the probability of successful decoding within at most two transmission occasions.

</details>


### [155] [Capacity of Uniform Noise Channels Under Average Input Power Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14352)
*Yihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文精确刻画了加性均匀噪声信道在平均输入功率约束下的容量，以及达到容量的输入与输出分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信息论将加性噪声信道容量表述为在二阶矩约束下最大化互信息的问题；但除高斯噪声外，即使是简单噪声分布的容量也缺乏定量理解。均匀噪声信道容量长期未被完全解决，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者利用均匀噪声信道输出密度的某种周期化恒等式，将问题转化为可用傅里叶分析处理的形式，并据此推导容量及容量达到分布的精确表征。

Result: 论文给出了中心区间均匀噪声信道容量的精确表达，并刻画了相应的容量达到输入分布和输出分布。

Conclusion: 通过发现输出密度的周期化结构并结合傅里叶分析，本文解决了均匀噪声加性信道容量这一长期问题，扩展了非高斯噪声信道容量理论的可精确分析范围。

Abstract: The foundational work of Shannon (1948) identified the capacity of an additive noise channel under an average input power constraint as a mutual information maximization problem over input densities subject to a second moment constraint. However, a quantitative understanding of the channel capacity is significantly lacking even for very simple noise distributions beyond Gaussians. In particular, it is a long standing question to determine the capacity of channels with noise uniformly distributed over a centered interval. This paper settles this question by precisely characterizing the capacity and the corresponding capacity achieving input and output distributions of such channels. A key observation en route to these results is a certain periodization identity for the output density of a uniform noise channel which in turn allows for applications of Fourier analytic techniques.

</details>


### [156] [Decision Making Needs Uncertainty Quantification [Lecture Notes]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14407)
*Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文从决策理论出发，说明不确定性表示应与决策目标和智能体知识状态相匹配，才能保证可靠且最优的行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多信号处理系统的最终目的不是单纯预测，而是采取行动；当决定行动的状态变量存在不确定性时，不确定性的表示方式会直接影响智能体的性能与可信度。

Method: 在统一的决策理论框架下，先分析环境分布已知时不同风险偏好的最优不确定性表示；再讨论环境未知时的认知不确定性处理方法，包括固定预测器校准、可信/歧义集合结合分布鲁棒优化，以及对模型参数进行贝叶斯推断。

Result: 结果表明，风险中性智能体需要状态的后验分布；风险规避智能体可用预测集和最坏情形决策规则而不损失最优性；环境未知时，不同方法分别提供可靠性校准、鲁棒性保证或参数不确定性建模。

Conclusion: 可靠决策依赖于与目标函数、风险偏好和知识条件相一致的不确定性表示，并需要相应保证来认证智能体实际可获得的效用。

Abstract: Many signal processing systems ultimately exist to {act}. Whenever the state variable that determines the action to be taken by a decision maker, or agent, is uncertain, the way that uncertainty is represented decides how well the agent performs and how much its performance can be trusted. This lecture note develops, from first principles and within a single decision-theoretic setting, the link between the {objective} and the knowledge of an agent and the form of uncertainty representation that is sufficient to act optimally. To start, assuming a known environment distribution, we show that a risk-neutral agent needs the posterior distribution over the state, whereas a risk-averse agent can rely without loss of optimality on a {prediction set} and a worst-case decision rule. We then turn to the case in which the environment is unknown, and identify three complementary approaches to address the resulting epistemic uncertainty: calibration of a fixed predictor, credal (ambiguity) sets with distributionally robust optimization, and Bayesian inference over model parameters. The common thread is that reliable decisions require an uncertainty representation matched to the decision objective and to the knowledge profile of the agent, together with a guarantee that certifies the utility the agent will actually obtain.

</details>


### [157] [Group Testing with Selectable Thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14448)
*Trung-Khang Tran,Daniel McMorrow,Jonathan Scarlett*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文研究一种可选择阈值的群测试模型，给出在大阈值与固定最大阈值情形下实现高概率恢复的测试数、速率及相应 converse 结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统群测试通常采用二值“是否至少含一个缺陷项”的检测规则。本文关注更一般的阈值测试：每次测试可选择阈值，并根据池中缺陷项数量是否达到阈值输出结果，以探索这种灵活性对信息效率和恢复能力的提升。

Method: 作者建立 selectable threshold group testing 模型，分析最大阈值很大或无界时速率接近 1 的可达条件；在固定最大阈值下，提出简单且计算高效的解码方案，并在测试设计满足正则性条件时证明 converse 下界。

Result: 当最大阈值足够大或无界时，可在高概率恢复下达到接近最优的速率 1；当最大阈值固定时，给出可达测试数与 converse，并证明二者在稠密极限 k=Θ(n^θ)、θ→1 时相吻合。

Conclusion: 可选择阈值显著扩展了群测试模型的表达能力。在合适阈值资源下，测试效率可接近信息论最优；在固定阈值限制下，简单解码仍能取得与理论下界在稠密极限中一致的性能。

Abstract: We consider the problem of group testing, in which one seeks to identify a subset of defective items of size $k$ from a larger set of $n$ items based on pooled tests. We introduce a selectable threshold model, in which each test has an associated threshold that can be chosen, such that the test outcome is 1 if and only if the number of defectives in the test is no smaller than that threshold. In settings with a large or unbounded maximum threshold, we establish conditions under which high-probability recovery can be attained with a rate (i.e., the asymptotic ratio of $\log_2{n \choose k}$ to the number of tests) approaching its maximum possible value of 1. Moreover, in the case of a fixed maximum threshold, we establish an achievable number of tests using simple and computationally efficient decoding methods, and a converse that holds under suitable regularity conditions on the test design, with the two coinciding in the dense limit (i.e., $θ$ approaching one in the scaling $k = Θ(n^θ)$).

</details>


### [158] [Quasi-Belief Propagation and Neural-Network Check Node Processing for BCH Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14589)
*Guangwen Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出面向 BCH 码的准 BP 解码及其神经网络变体，在保持并行化优势的同时降低复杂度并提升硬件友好性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 BCH 码译码在性能、吞吐率、时延和硬件实现复杂度之间存在权衡；BP 译码虽可并行，但校验节点中的双曲函数计算代价高，且 BCH 码的常规校验矩阵不一定能充分发挥 BP 性能。

Method: 提出 quasi-BP 译码框架，利用 BCH 码的自同构性质和优化的冗余校验矩阵增强 BP 译码效果；进一步用轻量级卷积神经网络替代校验节点更新中的 tanh 与 tanh^{-1} 运算，并采用三重约束损失函数以保证非负性与序关系一致性；还与有序统计译码变体进行级联。

Result: 在三个 BCH 码上的仿真表明，quasi-BP 的帧错误率性能具有竞争力，与相近码长 LDPC 码的 BP 译码相比差距在 0.25 dB 以内；神经网络变体性能损失可忽略，同时更适合硬件加速器上的稳定算术实现；与 OSD 变体级联后进一步接近最大似然界。

Conclusion: 所提方法为 BCH 码提供了一种兼具高吞吐、低时延、较强纠错性能和硬件友好性的译码方案，适用于下一代通信系统。

Abstract: This paper proposes a quasi-BP decoding scheme for BCH codes that preserves the parallelizable structure of belief propagation while exploiting code automorphisms and optimized redundant parity-check matrices. To eliminate the computationally expensive $\tanh$ and $\tanh^{-1}$ functions in check node updates, we further introduce a neural-network-based variant that replaces them with a lightweight convolutional neural network trained under a triple-constraint loss function enforcing non-negativity and order consistency. Simulation results for three BCH codes demonstrate that quasi-BP decoding achieves competitive frame error rate performance, with a gap within 0.25 decibels compared with belief propagation decoding of an LDPC code of similar blocklength. The neural-network-based variant incurs negligible performance loss while enabling stable deployment with arithmetic operations on hardware accelerators. Concatenation with an ordered statistics decoding variant further bridges the gap to the maximum-likelihood bound. Hence, the proposed schemes offer a viable path toward high-throughput, low-latency decoding of BCH codes in next-generation communication systems.

</details>


### [159] [Identification Codes and Post-Shannon Communication: Theory, Architectures, and Emerging Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14666)
*Wafa Labidi,Kumar Nilesh,Johannes Rosenberger,Juan Cabrera,Holger Boche,Christian Deppe,Frank H. P. Fitzek,Marc Geitz*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文综述识别编码：它不重构消息，而是判断特定消息是否发送；凭借可识别消息数随块长双指数增长的特性，适用于监测、告警、控制、语义通信和联合感知等无需完整解码的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统香农通信以完整消息重构为目标，但在监测、告警、控制等大规模分布式系统中，接收端往往只需判断某个特定消息是否被发送。识别编码以假设检验替代解码，为后香农通信和超大规模消息空间提供新的理论与系统架构基础。

Method: 本文是一篇综述，回顾 Ahlswede 和 Dueck 提出的识别编码理论基础，梳理其双指数可识别消息规模、适用边界与系统实现思路，并讨论多个新兴应用场景与工业实现案例。

Result: 综述指出，识别编码在块长增长时可支持双指数级别的可识别消息数量，适合无需完整消息恢复的任务；其原则可用于监测系统、专用数据存储、联合识别与感知、语义通信、移动网络控制和网络化共识测试等场景。

Conclusion: 识别编码不能普遍替代经典通信，但在特定任务导向型通信中具有显著优势，是后香农通信的重要组成部分，并有潜力支撑面向大规模分布式系统的新型通信架构。

Abstract: Identification (ID) coding, introduced by Ahlswede and Dueck, extends Shannon's classical communication paradigm by replacing message reconstruction with hypothesis testing. Instead of decoding the transmitted message, the receiver only decides whether a particular message was sent. A fundamental result of ID theory is the double-exponential growth in the number of identifiable messages with respect to (w.r.t.) the blocklength. This scaling behavior enables fundamentally new communication architectures for large-scale distributed systems and forms a key building block of post-Shannon communication.
  While ID cannot replace classical communication in general, it is particularly well-suited for scenarios in which full message reconstruction is unnecessary, such as monitoring, alarming, and control systems.
  In this survey, we review the theoretical foundations of ID coding and discuss emerging communication architectures and application domains based on this paradigm. Particular emphasis is placed on practical use cases, including monitoring systems, special-purpose data storage, joint identification and sensing (JIDAS), semantic communications, mobile-network control systems and networked consensus testing systems. We further highlight recent system concepts, industrial perspectives, and implementation examples that illustrate how ID-based principles can be realized in practical communication systems.

</details>


### [160] [Statistics of the Compression Ratio of a Variable-to-Variable Code: Exact Moments and Asymptotic Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14676)
*Neri Merhav*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究V2V长度码有限样本压缩率，给出所有整数阶矩的精确积分公式、比CLT更准的Edgeworth分布近似，以及偏差和方差常数的闭式表达，并将框架扩展到Markov源和有限状态编码器分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V2V长度码的有限样本压缩率会随机波动，且只在短语数趋于无穷时收敛到渐近速率；现有CLT近似难以精确刻画有限样本分布、偏差与方差常数，因此需要更精细的矩公式与分布近似。

Method: 针对离散无记忆源，推导压缩率R_n所有整数阶矩的精确闭式表达，将其表示为仅依赖单短语(L,ℓ)矩母函数的一维积分；进一步用这些矩构造Edgeworth CDF近似，并用Laplace积分法得到偏差常数和方差常数的显式公式。方法还扩展到Markov源，采用状态索引矩阵，并从有限状态编码器视角结合广义Kraft不等式分析码率下界。

Result: 得到E{R_n^k}的精确一维积分公式；Edgeworth近似显著优于CLT近似；给出n(E{R_n}-ρ)极限偏差常数C与n Var{R_n}极限方差常数的闭式表达；对Markov源得到闭式冗余公式；并揭示Khodak码性能提升对应更小的偏差常数。

Conclusion: 论文建立了V2V长度码有限样本压缩率的系统精确分析框架，既能刻画矩和分布，又能解释不同长度码结构的偏差来源，并说明偏差常数是评价实际压缩性能的重要指标。

Abstract: A variable-to-variable (V2V) length code parses a source sequence into phrases of variable length and maps each phrase to a binary codeword of, generally, a different random length. After encoding $n$ phrases, the realized compression ratio $R_n=Λ_n/Σ_n$ -- total codeword length over total source-symbol count -- is the finite-sample counterpart of the code's asymptotic rate $ρ$, to which it converges only as $n\to\infty$. This paper first derives exact formulas for all integer moments of $R_n$ for a given discrete memoryless source (DMS). Specifically, we obtain a closed-form formula for every moment $\E\{R_n^k\}$ as a one-dimensional integral involving only single-phrase moment generating functions of the pair $(L,\ell)$ -- the phrase length, in source symbols, and codeword length, in bits. From these moments we derive an Edgeworth approximation to the cumulative distribution function (CDF) of $R_n$ that is substantially more accurate than the central limit theorem (CLT) approximation. Using the Laplace method of integration, we also derive explicit closed-form formulas for the bias constant $C=\lim_{n\to\infty}n(\E\{R_n\}-ρ)$ and for the variance constant $\lim_{n\to\infty}n\cdot\Var\{R_n\}$. The analysis extends to Markov sources via state-indexed matrices with a redundancy formula obtained in closed form. On the coding-theoretic side, we cast V2V length codes as finite-state encoders and apply a generalized Kraft inequality for a compression-rate lower bound, and give a structural decomposition of the bias coefficient that separates cleanly across variable-to-fixed (V2F) length codes, fixed-to-variable (F2V) length codes, and V2V length codes. Applied to the Khodak code of Bugeaud, Drmota, and Szpankowski, this decomposition shows that its improved performance is reflected in its smaller bias constant.

</details>


### [161] [Random Spherical Codes at High SNR: Error Transitions, Fixed-Error Data Rates, and Converse Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14768)
*Nikola Zlatanov*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究固定块长、高信噪比且码本规模随信噪比增长时，实AWGN信道上的随机球面码本性能；发现误差概率存在由球面内在维度与码本增长尺度决定的锐阈值转变，并据此刻画可达速率与 converse 上界之间的渐近关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限块长与高信噪比条件下，理解随机球面码本的误差概率、可达速率及其与理论上界的差距，对于精细刻画非渐近通信性能具有重要意义。尤其需要明确码本规模随SNR增长时可靠性与速率之间的高SNR权衡。

Method: 作者分析固定块长下随机球面码本在实加性高斯白噪声信道中的集合平均误差概率，建立其随码本增长尺度变化的相变规律；再反演该误差转变律，得到给定误差概率下的高SNR可达速率展开，并与相应 converse rate bound 进行比较，进一步联合刻画块长与误差概率对极限速率间隙的影响。

Result: 误差概率在临界码本增长尺度处出现三段式行为：低于临界尺度时趋于0，等于临界尺度时收敛到非平凡极限，高于临界尺度时趋于1。可达速率与 converse 上界具有相同的高SNR主导增长项，因此二者比值趋于1；但其加性差距一般收敛到一个依赖块长与可靠性要求的正值。固定误差概率下，该差距随块长增大而消失。

Conclusion: 随机球面码本在高SNR、固定块长场景下具有清晰的误差相变结构，并在一阶意义上达到 converse 上界；但有限块长和可靠性要求会导致常数阶速率损失。若误差概率随块长下降过快，则该加性间隙在大块长极限下可能无法消失。

Abstract: This paper characterizes random spherical codebooks over the real additive white Gaussian noise channel in the high signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) regime when the blocklength is fixed and the codebook size grows with SNR. In this regime, the random spherical ensemble exhibits a sharp error-probability transition governed by the intrinsic dimension of the sphere and the codebook-growth scale. Below the critical codebook-growth scale, the ensemble-average error probability vanishes; at the critical scale, it converges to a nontrivial limit; and above that scale, it approaches one. By inverting this transition law, we obtain the high-SNR expansion of the ensemble-achievable data rate for a prescribed error probability. This rate has the same leading high-SNR growth as the corresponding converse rate bound, while reliability enters through the constant-order terms. Consequently, the ratio of the ensemble-achievable rate to the converse rate bound tends to one as the SNR increases. Their additive difference, however, generally approaches a positive blocklength- and reliability-dependent limit. We characterize this limiting rate-bound gap jointly as a function of blocklength and error probability. For every fixed error probability, the gap vanishes as the blocklength increases. We further identify the reliability scalings under which a decreasing error probability prevents the additive gap from vanishing in the large-blocklength limit.

</details>


### [162] [Lossy compression of weighted graph adjacency matrices by transform coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14834)
*Kenta Yanagiya,Junya Hara,Hiroshi Higashi,Yuichi Tanaka,Antonio Ortega*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出一种加权图压缩框架：拓扑无损传输，边权有损压缩；通过线图变换将边权视为线图上的图信号，并用图滤波器组、量化和熵编码实现压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权图中边权之间的相关关系不直观，直接对边权向量进行有损压缩难以有效利用结构相关性；需要一种能刻画边间关系并预测压缩难度的方法。

Method: 先将原无权图转换为线图，使原图的边对应线图节点，原图中相邻边关系对应线图边；随后把边权看作定义在线图节点上的图信号，使用线图上的图滤波器组进行变换，再对变换系数进行量化与熵编码。同时形式化定义线图上的边平滑性，并给出无需显式构造线图即可计算的平滑性度量。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，该方法相较已有矩阵预处理类方法具有更好的压缩效果，并验证了所提出平滑性度量可反映压缩难度。

Conclusion: 线图变换能有效揭示边权之间的结构相关性，使图信号处理工具可用于边权压缩；边平滑性可作为评估加权图压缩性能的有用指标。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a compression framework for weighted graphs in which the graph topology is transmitted losslessly and edge weights are compressed lossily. A challenge in the lossy compression of edge weights is that the underlying relationships between edges are ambiguous. To address this issue, we first transform the unweighted graph into the corresponding line graph, whose nodes represent the edges of the original graph and whose edges encode the relationships between them. The line graph transform allows us to regard edge weights as a graph signal defined on the line graph. Instead of transmitting the edge-weight vector, we first transform it with a graph filter bank on the line graph. Then, quantization and entropy coding are performed on the transformed coefficients of the edge weight vector. In addition to the lossy compression method, we formalize edge smoothness on the line graph and show that it serves as a measure of the difficulty of compression. The proposed smoothness measure can be easily calculated without converting to a line graph. This provides insight into the expected compression performance of a given weighted graph. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data validate the effectiveness of the proposed method by comparing it with existing matrix preprocessing methods.

</details>


### [163] [Covering Sequences and Covering-Sequences Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14840)
*Tuvi Etzion*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究覆盖序列及覆盖序列码的构造，利用汉明码在小覆盖半径下生成较短序列和较高效的码族，并给出渐近接近最优的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 覆盖序列的目标是让循环序列中连续的 n 元组形成具有给定覆盖半径 R 的长度 n 码。如何用更短的序列、更少的序列数量以及更少的总代码字实现良好覆盖，是组合编码理论中的核心问题。作者关注作为构造模块的 (n,m,R)-covering-sequences code，希望提升其构造效率并逼近理论最优。

Method: 论文基于汉明码的结构，针对小半径情形设计覆盖序列与覆盖序列码的构造方法；同时将该构造推广到素数幂大小字母表，并分析序列长度相对于最优界的渐近表现；此外，也考察同一构造在较大半径下产生的相关码。

Result: 作者证明汉明码可用于构造长度较短的覆盖序列，以及由较少循环序列组成、总代码字数量较小的 (n,m,R)-covering-sequences code。对于足够大的素数幂字母表，构造出的较小半径序列长度在渐近意义上接近最优；该方法在较大半径下也能得到具有一定价值的码构造。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了汉明码在覆盖序列构造中的有效性，尤其适用于小覆盖半径和较大素数幂字母表场景，为构造接近最优的覆盖序列及高效覆盖序列码提供了系统方法。

Abstract: An $(n,R)$-covering sequence is a cyclic sequence whose consecutive $n$-tuples form a code of length $n$ and covering radius $R$. An $(n,m,R)$-covering-sequences code is a set of cyclic sequences of length $m$, whose consecutive $n$-tuples form a code of length $n$ and covering radius $R$. These codes are the best building blocks for $(n,R)$-covering sequences. We show, for small radii, how the Hamming code can be used to construct such sequences of short length and such codes with a relatively small number of sequences and a total number of codewords. Sequences with small radius whose length approaches asymptotically to optimality are constructed, especially for an alphabet of prime power size large enough. With the same construction, interesting codes are also constructed for larger radii.

</details>


### [164] [Quantum XYZ Stabilizer Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14988)
*Alessio Baldelli,Davide Orsucci,Francisco Lázaro,Massimo Battaglioni*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出量子XYZ稳定子码框架，用三组两两正交的二进制校验矩阵分别构造X、Y、Z型稳定子，刻画其何时退化为CSS码，并给出最小距离界与有限长度qLDPC构造及仿真优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CSS稳定子码结构便利，但在有限码长下可能存在低重量逻辑算符与译码性能限制；引入非CSS约束有望抑制低重量逻辑算符并改善退相干噪声下的译码表现。

Method: 构造由X、Y、Z三类稳定子生成元组成的XYZ稳定子码；分析Y型校验是否冗余以及稳定子群是否存在CSS生成集；建立代数条件与秩条件判别“真非CSS”性质；推导量子最小距离及混合Pauli逻辑算符的上下界；基于相交子集与准二元/准二面体类码族构造稀疏qLDPC实例，并用四元置信传播译码进行仿真。

Result: 该框架包含已知的非CSS拓扑码XYZ²六角码，并产生若干稀疏有限长度XYZ qLDPC码；在退极化码容量噪声与四元BP译码下，所提XYZ qLDPC实例相较参数相近的代表性CSS qLDPC实例表现更优。

Conclusion: XYZ稳定子码为系统构造和判别真非CSS量子码提供了统一框架；额外Y型稳定子约束可在有限长度场景改善逻辑距离结构与译码性能，显示出非CSS qLDPC码设计的潜力。

Abstract: Stabilizer codes are often constructed within the Calderbank--Shor--Steane (CSS) framework, where two mutually orthogonal binary classical codes define $X$ and $Z$-type stabilizer generators. While this structure is algebraically convenient, additional non-CSS constraints may help suppress low-weight logical operators and improve decoding performance in the finite-length regime. We thus introduce quantum XYZ stabilizer codes, whose parity-check matrix (PCM) is built from three pairwise orthogonal binary PCMs associated with $X$-, $Y$-, and $Z$-type stabilizer generators. A nontrivial point is that an XYZ code instance is not automatically genuinely non-CSS: the same stabilizer group may admit a CSS generating set. We characterize this collapse, obtaining algebraic and rank conditions for deciding when the $Y$-type checks are redundant and when they define genuinely non-CSS stabilizer constraints. We also derive upper and lower bounds on the quantum minimum distance, including bounds for mixed Pauli logical operators. The novel framework includes a known non-CSS topological code, namely the XYZ$^2$ hexagonal code, and yields also sparse finite-length quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) constructions from intersecting-subset and quasi-dyadic code families. Simulations under depolarizing code-capacity noise and quaternary belief propagation decoding show that the proposed XYZ qLDPC instances can outperform representative CSS qLDPC instances with similar finite-length parameters.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Intelligent Three Level Learning Architecture for Autonomous UAV Swarms in Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14093)
*Oleksii Bychkov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向自主无人机群搜索救援任务的三层分层学习架构，将个体适应、战术协同与战略决策分别对应到不同学习机制，并通过形式化契约给出多类保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层强化学习或群体智能方法通常在不同层级使用单一学习范式，难以同时处理个体快速适应、多智能体协同、战略推理与系统级安全/一致性保证。论文旨在构建一种更贴近生物层级认知的混合神经符号架构，以提升搜索救援场景中的可靠性、可解释性和适应性。

Method: 提出三层分层学习体系：底层使用Hebbian神经可塑性支持个体智能体适应；中层结合多智能体强化学习、图神经网络与行为树实现战术协同；高层采用模型无关元学习、BDI推理与数字孪生进行战略决策。进一步将架构形式化为六个组件上的二十二个架构契约，并定义群体元认知、五类任务进展函数、静态与动态场景下的保证条件及集成定理。

Result: 理论上证明：当所有契约满足时，该混合神经符号系统可保持安全性、预算正确性、最优性、活性、无饥饿性与层间一致性六类保证；在动态主动学习场景下，新增契约进一步支持认知韧性、优雅降级和单调元改进等保证。

Conclusion: 该工作主要是一个理论与架构层面的贡献，试图通过异构学习机制、形式化契约和元认知机制，解决现有分层强化学习在可保证性、跨层一致性、动态适应与任务落地方面的局限。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel three level hierarchical learning architecture for autonomous UAV swarms performing search and rescue operations. Unlike conventional approaches that apply a single learning paradigm across all hierarchy levels, the proposed architecture integrates three qualitatively different learning mechanisms corresponding to the biological hierarchy of reflexes, skills, and reasoning such as Hebbian neuroplasticity for individual agent adaptation, multi agent reinforcement learning with graph neural networks and behavior trees for tactical coordination, and model agnostic meta learning with BDI reasoning and a digital twin for strategic decision making.
  The architecture is formalized through twenty two architectural contracts organized across six components such as BDI, Behavior Trees, GNN, MARL, Neuroplasticity, Meta Learning that collectively provide six classes of formal guarantees such as safety, budget correctness, optimality, liveness, starvation freedom, and inter level consistency.
  We introduce Swarm Meta Cognition as a compositional property arising from the structured interaction of all three levels, enabling the swarm to monitor its own cognitive state and switch between cognitive strategies. Five constructive progress functions for SAR task types bridge the gap between abstract optimization theory and concrete operational scenarios.
  The main integration theorem establishes that when all contracts are satisfied, the hybrid neuro-symbolic system preserves all six guarantee classes. For the dynamic case with active learning, five new contracts extend the framework with three additional guarantees such as cognitive resilience, graceful degradation, and monotonic meta improvement. Theoretical analysis demonstrates that the architecture addresses five fundamental limitations of existing hierarchical RL approaches.

</details>


### [166] [HG-RAG: Hierarchy-Guided Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Structured Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14095)
*Pranav Yadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HG-RAG：基于层次知识图谱遍历的检索增强生成框架，相比平面稠密检索在层次、关系与多跳推理任务上表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG通常从平面文档库中检索上下文，难以处理需要层级结构理解或跨关系推理的查询，因此需要一种能利用结构化知识关系的检索机制。

Method: 构建层次化知识图谱；从查询中解析命名实体锚点；围绕该锚点进行图遍历检索，包括向上扩展父节点、横向扩展关系邻居，并在需要时向下扩展子节点；将结构化上下文提供给语言模型生成答案。

Result: 在18到800个节点的三种世界规模、四类查询任务（局部事实、层次、邻域、多跳）上，与稠密检索基线相比，HG-RAG在层次、关系和多跳推理任务中持续优于平面基线，同时减少幻觉并保持局部一致性。

Conclusion: 层次引导的图遍历检索能弥补平面RAG在结构化推理中的不足，是提升复杂查询回答质量和可靠性的有效方向。

Abstract: Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) has proven to be a widely successful process at improving the quality of outputs from a Large Language Model (LLM) for wider context. However, RAG systems typically retrieve context from flat document stores, which struggles when queries require hierarchical or relational reasoning across structured knowledge. I present HG-RAG (Hierarchy-Guided RAG), a framework that performs graph-traversal over a hierarchical knowledge graph to deliver structured context to a language model. My retrieval pipeline resolves a named entity anchor from the query, then expands context upward through parent nodes, laterally through relational neighbors, and downward through child nodes when needed. I evaluate HG-RAG against a dense retrieval baseline across three world scales (18-800 nodes) with four query types: local fact, hierarchical, neighborhood, and multi-hop. Results show HG-RAG consistently outperforms the flat baseline on hierarchical, relational, and multi-hop reasoning tasks, while reducing hallucination and maintaining locality coherence.

</details>


### [167] [IMEX Interaction-Based Model Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14096)
*Emiliano Massi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍 IMEX 可解释建模方法，用 PCS 衡量单特征重要性、PCI 捕捉特征交互；实验验证 PCS 在复杂特征关系下能有效识别关键变量结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱预测模型虽然可能具有较高准确率，但其内部决策机制不透明，尤其在关键应用场景中，仅凭预测准确性不足以验证模型可信度。因此，论文关注如何解释模型为何产生特定预测，并识别影响预测的关键变量及变量间交互。

Method: 提出/采用 IMEX（Interaction-Based Model Explanation）可解释预测建模框架。该方法通过两个互补指标进行解释：PCS（Static Correlation Power）用于衡量单个特征对目标预测的贡献；PCI（Interaction Correlation Power）用于捕捉特征之间的非加性互动效应。本文重点对 PCS 组件进行实验验证，并将其与 INVASE 在三个具有已知结构的合成数据集上比较。

Result: 实验结果表明，IMEX 的 PCS 组件能够在存在非线性、条件依赖以及多重共线性关系的情况下，恢复与目标预测相关的特征级结构，并识别重要变量。

Conclusion: IMEX 为黑箱预测模型提供了一种基于变量贡献和交互关系的解释路径。其 PCS 指标在合成实验中表现出识别关键特征结构的能力，说明该框架有潜力用于构建预测结果的可解释性映射，并支持对潜在机制的探索。

Abstract: In predictive modeling, the ability to explain why a model produces a given target prediction has become increasingly important [5, 10]. Black-box models do not provide a transparent description of the internal mechanisms that generate the prediction, making even accurate predictions difficult to interpret and validate. In critical contexts, predictive accuracy alone is not a sufficient validation metric if the reasons underlying model decisions remain unexplained. The IMEX (Interaction-Based Model Explanation) approach represents a methodological direction within explainable predictive modeling. IMEX is designed to identify which variables contribute most to the target prediction and which interactions among variables are significant in determining the target. The method does not impose limitations on higher-order interaction analysis, allowing the investigation of feature subsets with cardinality greater than two. Beyond the identification of feature importance, IMEX enables the exploration of interaction patterns that may be consistent with latent mechanisms influencing the outcome. Through the application of the IMEX algorithm, it is possible to construct an interpretability map of the predictions. The IMEX framework is built on two complementary metrics: Static Correlation Power (PCS), which quantifies the contribution of individual features, and Interaction Correlation Power (PCI), which captures non-additive effects among features. In the present work, the PCS component is experimentally validated through a comparison with INVASE [18] on three synthetic datasets with known structures. The results indicate that IMEX can recover relevant feature-level structures in the presence of non-linear, conditional, and multicollinear relationships between input features and prediction targets.

</details>


### [168] [RegNetAgents: A Multi-Agent Framework for Cross-Network Regulatory Driver Identification in Cancer Genomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14097)
*Jose A. Bird*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RegNetAgents 是一个面向癌症基因组学的多智能体分析框架，用于在异质基因调控网络中识别候选调控因子，并显示出对癌症相关基因的显著富集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肿瘤 bulk 数据网络与单细胞调控网络来源异质、难以统一比较和解释；研究希望构建一个可查询、可解释的下游分析层，将预计算调控网络整合起来，用于发现与癌症相关的调控候选因子。

Method: 框架整合 TCGA 衍生癌症 ARACNe 网络与 GREmLN 大规模单细胞调控网络；针对焦点基因进行双网络分类、基于 OncoKB 的癌症基因过滤、肿瘤网络调控关系的作用模式分配，并按跨网络证据一致性对候选因子排序。系统以 LangGraph DAG 多智能体工作流实现，并通过 Python API 与 MCP 客户端访问。

Result: 在 BRCA 的 11 个和 COAD 的 12 个焦点基因上，候选调控因子显著富集 OncoKB 注释癌症基因。TCGA 候选因子富集显著，BRCA Stouffer Z=6.69、COAD Z=6.95；GREmLN 候选因子同样显著，BRCA Z=5.51、COAD Z=7.06，均 p<0.0001。管家基因和非驱动对照未见富集。

Conclusion: RegNetAgents 提供了一个可解释、结构化的 AI 框架，可在跨网络癌症调控分析中识别候选调控因子，并支持从候选发现到生物学假设生成的端到端解释。

Abstract: We introduce RegNetAgents, an AI-oriented multi-agent framework for structured, query-driven regulatory candidate identification across heterogeneous gene regulatory networks. The system enables unified analysis of bulk tumor and single-cell-derived ARACNe networks by integrating TCGA-derived cancer networks with large-scale single-cell regulatory networks from the GREmLN project. For a given focal gene, the framework performs dual-network classification, cancer gene filtering using OncoKB annotations, and mode-of-action (MoA) assignment for tumor-derived regulatory relationships. Candidates are ranked by evidence consistency across networks (Both, TCGA-only, GREmLN-only). The system is implemented as a multi-agent LangGraph DAG workflow, accessible through a unified Python API and Model Context Protocol (MCP) client, operating as a downstream analytical layer over precomputed regulatory networks rather than a network inference method. Across eleven breast cancer (BRCA) and twelve colorectal cancer (COAD) focal genes, RegNetAgents identifies candidate regulators significantly enriched for OncoKB-annotated cancer genes. TCGA-derived candidates show strong enrichment (Stouffer Z = 6.69 for BRCA and 6.95 for COAD), while GREmLN-derived candidates also demonstrate significant enrichment (Z = 5.51 for BRCA and 7.06 for COAD; all p < 0.0001). No enrichment is observed in housekeeping or non-driver control gene sets, supporting signal specificity. An extended module enables structured evaluation of oncogenic potential, druggability, clinical relevance, and network vulnerability, supporting end-to-end interpretation from candidate identification to biological hypothesis generation. RegNetAgents establishes an interpretable AI framework for cross-network regulatory candidate identification in cancer genomics.

</details>


### [169] [DialogueVPR: Towards Conversational Visual Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14115)
*Yukun Song,Changwei Wang,Xingtian Pei,Shibiao Xu,Wenhao Xu,Shunpeng Chen,Yu Zhang,Ke Zhang,Rongtao Xu,Xuxiang Feng,Pengyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dialogue Place Recognition（DlgPR），将语言引导地理定位从静态一次检索转为交互式对话推理检索，并构建首个大规模对话式地点识别基准 DlgQuest-Cities。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言引导地理定位方法多采用静态、一次性检索范式，难以处理真实自然语言描述中的歧义、不完整和信息不足问题；人类在传递空间信息时通常通过追问与澄清逐步缩小范围，因此需要一种交互式推理定位范式。

Method: 作者提出 DlgPR 任务与统一推理框架，结合跨模态多层级检索器和智能提问器 DQ-pilot。DQ-pilot 采用课程式训练：先在 DQ-cities-20k 子集上监督微调，再在更难的 DQ-cities-10k 上通过 GRPO 强化优化。训练中引入 Discriminative Difficulty Index（DDI）用于课程采样，以及 Positional Retrieval Gain（PRG）作为衡量问题带来检索改进的奖励。

Result: 实验表明，基于对话推理的 DlgPR 方法显著优于传统静态检索和相关基线方法，证明主动提问能够有效提升地点识别性能。

Conclusion: 该工作推动语言引导地理定位从被动匹配走向主动推理，通过对话式信息补全提升了复杂真实场景下的定位鲁棒性，并提供了新的数据集、任务定义和训练框架。

Abstract: Inspired by how humans communicate spatial information, language-guided geo-localization has gained significant traction for its intuitive and practical value. Despite this progress, most methods still rely on a static, one-shot retrieval paradigm, which fails to handle the ambiguity and incompleteness inherent in real-world natural language descriptions. We propose a paradigm shift to reasoning retrieval and introduce Dialogue Place Recognition (DlgPR), which casts localization as an interactive, dialogue-driven reasoning process. To support this new task, we present DlgQuest-Cities, the first large-scale dialogue-based benchmark for place recognition, and a unified reasoning framework that couples a cross-modal multi-level retriever with an intelligent questioner, DQ-pilot. DQ-pilot is trained in a curriculum: supervised fine-tuning on a curated DQ-cities-20k subset followed by reinforcement refinement on a harder DQ-cities-10k split via GRPO. Two task-aligned metrics guide learning: a Discriminative Difficulty Index (DDI) for curriculum sampling and a Positional Retrieval Gain (PRG) reward that directly measures retrieval improvement induced by a question. Experiments show this reasoning-based approach significantly outperforms baselines. The code and model are available at https://github.com/Graysonggg/DlgPR.

</details>


### [170] [Interpretable Language Model for Closed-Loop Type 1 Diabetes Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14126)
*Maya Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 LLM-T1D：将强化学习胰岛素控制策略蒸馏到大语言模型中，在保持可解释性的同时实现较好的血糖控制与安全验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的人工胰腺系统可自动调节胰岛素输注，但其黑箱特性降低了患者和医生的信任度；因此需要一种既具备控制性能又能解释决策依据的透明控制器。

Method: 先训练专家强化学习控制器，再将其策略知识蒸馏到微调后的 LLaMA 3.1 8B 与 Qwen3 8B 模型中，使大语言模型根据状态生成胰岛素控制决策及自然语言解释；并在 FDA 批准的 UVA/Padova T1D 模拟器上测试，同时进行形式化安全验证以防止幻觉导致不安全输出。

Result: LLM 控制器在模拟环境中达到 73.5% Time in Range，且据摘要称性能超过原专家强化学习系统，并能以通俗语言解释控制决策，同时满足严格安全验证要求。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，大语言模型可作为可解释的胰岛素泵控制器，通过蒸馏强化学习策略兼顾性能、透明度与安全性，为可信人工胰腺系统提供了新方向。

Abstract: Type 1 Diabetes (T1D) is a chronic, life-threatening autoimmune condition characterized by the complete destruction of insulin-producing pancreatic beta cells. While Artificial Pancreas Systems (APS) powered by Reinforcement Learning (RL) have shown promise in automating insulin delivery, their ``black-box'' nature makes it hard for patients and doctors to trust them fully. This paper presents LLM-T1D, a promising approach that combines the precision of RL with the clear, human-like reasoning of Large Language Models (LLMs) to create a more transparent and reliable insulin pump controller. By training an expert RL system and distilling its knowledge into fine-tuned LLaMA 3.1 8B and Qwen3 8B models, we developed a controller that not only surpasses the RL system's performance but also explains its decisions in plain, understandable language. Tested on the FDA-approved UVA/Padova T1D simulator, the LLM controllers deliver excellent blood sugar control (73.5% Time in Range) while maintaining strict formal safety verification against hallucinations.

</details>


### [171] [Human AI Construction of Bayesian Networks for Operational Decision Support -- A Virtual Survey Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14141)
*Kumar Rahul,Shovan Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用大语言模型代理群辅助构建贝叶斯信念网络，通过多角色概率估计与截尾均值降噪，弥合专家判断与数据驱动学习之间的缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯信念网络适合不确定性决策，但网络结构构建与参数估计困难；现有方法通常依赖专家判断或大规模数据，二者各有局限，因此需要一种在专家知识与数据学习之间折中的建模方法。

Method: 作者提出一个六步BBN框架，利用具有特定人格设定与上下文的多AI代理面板估计条件概率，并采用截尾均值规则过滤噪声响应；随后将该方法应用于替代性医疗系统中顾客就医咨询意向的建模。

Result: 案例显示，自我效能表面上是重要因素，但其实际因果影响较小；相比之下，主观规范对顾客意向建模具有更强影响。同时，同时提升顾客信心与社区规范是最有效策略。

Conclusion: 大语言模型代理可作为BBN结构与参数估计的辅助来源，尤其适用于专家知识有限或数据不足的场景；该方法能支持因果洞察与策略评估，但仍需进一步验证其可靠性、偏差控制和可推广性。

Abstract: Bayesian Belief Networks (BBNs) are powerful tools for decision-making under uncertainty. However, building their structures and estimating parameters are difficult. Currently, researchers must choose between relying on expert judgement or using large datasets to learn the structure and parameters of the network. We propose a new methodology using Large Language Models to bridge the gap between expert opinion and data-driven learning. This approach uses a panel of AI agents to estimate probabilities based on specific personas and context. We then apply a trimmed-mean rule to remove noise from these responses. We develop a six step BBN framework and illustrate it to model customer intention to consult a doctor in an alternative healthcare system. The model reveals that while self efficacy appears to be a major factor, its actual causal impact is small. In contrast, subjective norms have a much stronger effect in modelling customers' intention. The most effective strategy is to improve both confidence and community norms simultaneously.

</details>


### [172] [Capability from Access Structure, Not Scale: Lower Bounds and Pre-Registered Tests for Hybrid Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14144)
*Wenhui Chen,Jianlin Chen,Ziyao Lin,Chi Man Vong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出能力收敛假说（CCH）：即使大模型表征趋同，在固定逐 token 推理预算下，能力也不会自动趋同；能力需要特定访问结构，尤其是同时具备压缩状态通道与可扩展逐字索引通道的混合架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应并限定柏拉图表征假说（PRH）：PRH强调模型规模增大后异构网络的表征会趋向共同现实模型，但作者认为表征相似并不等同于任务能力相同，尤其在长程检索、状态跟踪和组合推理等受资源约束的场景中。

Method: 提出CCH理论框架，以无限流中的“牛顿的苹果”见证任务为锚点，定义三类资源壁垒：Shannon wall、horizon wall、circuit wall；在显式可分性假设下分析混合架构如何跨越这些壁垒，并通过预注册的小规模实验验证预测。

Result: 实验观察到预期的“剪刀差”：精确检索错误率从0.994降至0.000，当64标量状态加入一层全局注意力后显著改善；状态跟踪分岔出现在预注册边界；合取见证任务显示需要不可约的双通道解法；另有一项预测失败且方向相反，作者如实报告。

Conclusion: 表征收敛可以随规模自然出现，但能力收敛不能免费获得，必须通过访问结构来“购买”；具有压缩状态通道与可扩展逐字索引通道的 access-complete hybrid 是作者认为能力趋同的关键架构类别。

Abstract: The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) holds that as models scale, representations of heterogeneous networks converge toward a shared model of reality. We propose its sequel and boundary, the Capability Convergence Hypothesis (CCH): under a fixed per-token inference budget, representational convergence does not entail capability convergence. Capability instead converges toward a class, the access-complete hybrid: any architecture holding both a compressive O(1)-state channel and a scalable verbatim-index channel. We anchor it on a witness task, the Newton's-apple problem in an infinite stream, and name three resource walls: a Shannon wall barring any o(Nb)-state architecture, a horizon wall barring any fixed window, and a circuit wall barring fixed-depth attention-only composition (conditional on TC0 != NC1). Under an explicit separability assumption a hybrid crosses all three by paying each wall's price, so capability is strictly super-additive under composition. We separate what we prove from what we conjecture: the access-completeness principle rests on information-theoretic lower bounds and pre-registered experiments, while the field-level convergence trend is an economics-motivated conjecture. We report the first pre-registered small-scale tests under criteria frozen before the data: the predicted scissors gap is measured (exact-retrieval error 0.994 vs. 0.000 once a 64-scalar state gains one global-attention layer), the state-tracking bifurcation lands at the registered boundary, and a conjunction witness shows an irreducibly two-channel solution; one prediction failed with its direction reversed and is reported as such. Representational convergence is given freely by scale; capability convergence must be purchased by access structure.

</details>


### [173] [ToolAnchor: Anchoring Counterfactual Context to Boost Agentic Tool-use Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14145)
*Weiting Liu,Jieyi Bi,Wanqi Zhou,Jianfeng Feng,Yining Ma,Ai Han,Wenlian Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ToolAnchor，用反事实锚定上下文缓解工具增强型 LLM Agent 在扩展新工具时的“行为惯性”，并通过教师生成、学生验证与后训练内化来提升动态适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强型大语言模型智能体通常在固定工具集上后训练；当任务需要引入新工具时，智能体倾向继续使用熟悉工具和既有推理模式，难以有效利用新工具，而从头重训成本高且不现实。

Method: 作者将问题归因于 behavioral inertia，并提出在关键决策点注入 counterfactual anchor contexts 以打破惯性。ToolAnchor 框架使用教师模型生成候选反事实上下文，通过学生模型 rollout 验证其有效性，再将成功干预样本用于 agentic post-training，使智能体内化新工具使用策略。

Result: 在 GAIA、BrowseComp 和 VDR-Bench 等通用助手、文本搜索与视觉搜索任务上，ToolAnchor 在扩展工具集场景下表现稳定，并取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: ToolAnchor 连接了静态后训练与动态工具适应之间的缺口，为可扩展的智能体强化学习与工具集扩展提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language model agents excel at long-horizon tasks, yet they are typically post-trained on fixed toolsets. When tasks demand new tools, these agents struggle to incorporate them effectively, and retraining from scratch is often impractical. We identify the core obstacle in such toolset expansion problem as behavioral inertia: the tendency of agents to fall back on familiar tools and established reasoning patterns despite having access to new ones. We demonstrate that injecting counterfactual anchor contexts at critical decision points can break this inertia, recovering failed trajectories by eliciting suppressed agent capabilities. To scale this insight, we propose ToolAnchor, a framework that uses teacher models to hypothesize these counterfactual contexts, verifies them via student rollouts, and internalizes the successful interventions through agentic post-training. Extensive evaluations across general AI assistant (GAIA), textual search (BrowseComp), and visual search (VDR-Bench) tasks demonstrate that ToolAnchor consistently exhibits competitive performance under expanded toolsets. Our work bridges the gap between static post-training and dynamic adaptation, charting a new path for scalable agentic reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [174] [Enhancing Small Language Models Reasoning through Knowledge Graph Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14149)
*Dimitrios Kelesis,Konstantinos Bougiatiotis,Georgios Paliouras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究用神经符号代理框架增强小语言模型在多跳亲属关系推理任务上的能力，发现RGCN提示能显著提升性能，但系统受限于事实抽取错误与多步推理误差累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型零样本推理能力强，但部署成本高且环境负担大；小语言模型更可持续，但在复杂、多跳、需要逻辑 grounding 的任务上容易出错。因此，作者希望探索低资源条件下，如何借助符号工具与专家模型提升小模型推理能力。

Method: 作者以Gemma 3（1B、4B）和Llama 3.2（3B）为基础，在CLUTRR亲属关系基准上构建极简神经符号代理。代理使用两个工具：extract_facts用于从故事中抽取符号三元组，get_hint通过关系图卷积网络（RGCN）提供专家推理提示。实验包含Oracle场景（使用真实三元组）和Realistic场景（依赖模型自抽取事实），并与仅使用故事输入的基线比较。

Result: RGCN生成的提示相较story-only基线带来约1.5到2倍性能提升。然而，在现实场景中，事实抽取质量成为主要瓶颈；早期抽取错误会在多跳推理链中累积。同时，部分模型架构出现“干扰效应”：带噪声的自生成事实即使配合专家提示，也可能降低性能。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，神经符号代理能有效提升小语言模型的多跳推理表现，但可靠的符号 grounding 仍是关键挑战。未来需要引入迭代验证、错误修正和更鲁棒的事实抽取机制，以构建更稳定的低资源神经符号推理系统。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) have set benchmarks for zero-shot reasoning, their deployment remains cost-prohibitive and environmentally taxing. Small Language Models (SLMs) offer a sustainable alternative, but prone to errors, on tasks requiring complex, multi-hop logical grounding. We investigate a neuro-symbolic agentic framework to enhance the reasoning capabilities of SLMs, specifically Gemma 3 (1B, 4B) and Llama 3.2 (3B), using the CLUTRR kinship benchmark. Our approach transforms the SLM into a minimalist agent utilizing two specialized tool calls: extract_facts for symbolic triplet extraction and get_hint for expert reasoning via a Relational Graph Convolutional Network (RGCN). We evaluate these models across two configurations, both in an Oracle scenario with ground-truth triplets and a Realistic scenario relying on self-extracted knowledge. Our results reveal that while RGCN-derived hints provide a 1.5 - 2x performance gain over story-only baselines, the system is constrained by the extraction bottleneck and sequential deductive fragility, where early extraction errors compound over multi-hop chains. Furthermore, we identify a "distraction effect" in specific architectures where noisy, self-generated facts degrade performance despite the presence of expert hints. This work characterizes the challenges of symbolic grounding in low-resource agentic systems and provides a roadmap for iterative verification in neuro-symbolic agentic pipelines.

</details>


### [175] [Orchestrating Power Grid Studies with Multi-Agent AI and MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14158)
*Jérôme Picault,Clément Goubet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨 Agentic AI 与 Model Context Protocol（MCP）如何在输电系统运营商（TSO）的电网研究场景中，连接大语言模型、仿真工具、结构化流程与人工监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电网研究依赖复杂数值仿真与专业流程，传统工具交互成本高、自动化与可审计性不足；作者希望通过智能体辅助提升仿真设置、分析执行、结果检索和工作流协作效率。

Method: 论文提出工业场景下智能体辅助电网研究的关键需求，并介绍 pypowsybl-mcp：一个基于 MCP 的接口，将 pypowsybl 仿真工具的部分能力以标准化工具调用形式暴露给 AI 智能体。同时讨论人类在环、多智能体工作流原则和评估策略。

Result: 该工作形成了一个初步测试平台，使智能体能够通过标准接口与电力系统仿真器交互，完成仿真配置、执行分析和结果获取等任务，并为后续技术指标与从业者反馈评估奠定基础。

Conclusion: 作者认为，基于 MCP 的工具集成是构建更具交互性、可审计性和可扩展性的电网研究环境的重要一步，但仍处于定位/概念验证阶段，需要进一步实验验证与行业实践反馈。

Abstract: This position paper explores how Agentic AI and Model Context Protocol (MCP) can support power-grid studies in a Transmission System Operator (TSO) context. We focus on integrating Large Language Models with numerical simulation tools, structured workflows, and human supervision. We identify key industrial requirements for agent assisted grid studies and introduce pypowsybl-mcp, an MCP-based interface exposing selected capabilities of our simulation tool, pypowsybl to AI agents. This first step provides a testbed to study how agents can setup simulations, execute analyses, retrieve results, and interact with power-system simulators through standardized tool calls. We also discuss principles for human-in-the-loop, multi-agent workflows and outline an evaluation strategy combining technical metrics and practitioner feedback. The paper positions MCP-based tool integration as a step toward more interactive, auditable, and scalable grid-study environments.

</details>


### [176] [MemoHarness: Agent Harnesses That Learn from Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14159)
*Yue Huang,Wenjie Wang,Han Bao,Yuchen Ma,Xiaonan Luo,Yi Nian,Haomin Zhuang,Zheyuan Liu,Yue Zhao,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemoHarness 是一种自适应智能体 harness 优化框架，通过从历史执行经验中学习并按测试案例检索适配控制配置，使 LLM 智能体相比固定 harness 获得更好的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动优化方法多聚焦于提示词、流程或工作流等局部对象，而实际部署的智能体往往对所有任务复用同一个全局 harness；然而 harness 作为管理上下文、工具、编排、记忆、解码与输出处理的外部控制层，会显著影响智能体行为，因此需要一种可根据任务自适应调整的优化方法。

Method: 论文提出 MemoHarness，将 harness 分解为六个可编辑控制维度；构建双层经验库，保存单案例诊断与提炼出的全局模式；在测试时无需标签、反馈或额外搜索，通过检索相关经验来为每个测试案例适配 learned harness。

Result: 在 shell-agent、代码生成和分析推理基准上，MemoHarness 优于所比较的固定 harness，并在未见过的测试套件和基础模型上表现出选择性迁移能力；当检索经验大部分可缓存时，其额外上下文成本仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 执行经验可作为构建自适应 agent harness 的实用基础，使其相比单一静态配置更灵活有效；但统计稳健性和各组件归因等更广泛结论仍需未来研究进一步验证。

Abstract: An agent harness is the external control layer that turns a base LLM into an executable agent by managing context, tools, orchestration, memory, decoding, and output handling. While harness design strongly affects agent behavior, most automatic improvement methods optimize narrower artifacts such as prompts, pipelines, or workflows, and deployed agents usually reuse a single global harness for all cases. We introduce MemoHarness, an adaptive harness optimization framework that learns from its own executions. MemoHarness decomposes the harness into six editable control dimensions, stores per-case diagnoses and distilled global patterns in a dual-layer experience bank, and adapts the learned harness to each test case using retrieved experience without test-time labels, feedback, or additional search. In our evaluation across shell-agent, code-generation, and analytical-reasoning benchmarks, MemoHarness improves over the fixed harnesses we compare against and shows selective transfer to unseen suites and base models. Its additional context can also remain cost-competitive when much of the retrieved experience is cacheable. These results provide evidence that execution experience is a practical substrate for building agent harnesses that are more adaptive than a single static configuration, while leaving broader claims about statistical robustness and component attribution to future work.

</details>


### [177] [When a Verified World Model Still Loses: Play-Adequacy vs Prediction-Accuracy in LLM-Synthesized Code World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14169)
*Javier Aguilar Martín*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Large language models can synthesize a game's rules as executable code - a Code World Model (CWM) - which a classical planner then searches over. Such models are typically accepted when they reach high transition accuracy on sampled trajectories. We argue this is the wrong notion of adequacy for planning.
  We show four things. (1) An LLM-synthesized CWM can pass a sampling gate at 100% transition accuracy and be $\geq 98\%$ state-accurate on the planner's own search distribution, yet lose systematically at play, because the $<1\%$ it gets wrong is exactly the pivotal dynamics; the play cost of the omitted rule is $0.091$ (seed-clustered 95% CI $[0.065,0.117]$, $n=4800$). We call this the verified-vs-correct gap, and confirm it end-to-end through the synthesis pipeline. (2) The harm follows a quantitative law, $\mathrm{danger}=\mathrm{play\_cost}\times(1-\mathrm{rarity})^N$, whose $(1-\mathrm{rarity})^N$ gate-miss factor is proven exact and whose play cost is empirically bounded. (3) The failure is not repaired by more data: LLM synthesis behaves as rule translation, not rule inference, and did not infer the omitted rule across models (GPT-5.x) and data regimes (including DAgger and targeted examples).
